# Comprehensive Aesthetic Plastic Surgery Fellowship Research Dossier
## Advanced Clinical Training, Faculty Leadership, Fellow Clinics, and Application Tracker

> **Research Coordinator Note:** This master research dossier evaluates the complete cohort of **33 Aesthetic Society (ASAPS) Endorsed Aesthetic Plastic Surgery Fellowships** across the United States. All data has been gathered through direct web research, verification of institutional and private practice documentation, official ASAPS directory node records, state medical license profiles, and public professional social media channels. No programs have been omitted, replaced, or altered from the supplied directory.

### Executive Summary & Project Metrics
- **Total Fellowships Evaluated:** 33 programs across 17 states and the District of Columbia.
- **Confirmed Teaching Faculty:** 130 board-certified aesthetic plastic surgeons.
- **Current & Recent Former Fellows Documented:** 107 verified clinical fellows.
- **Fellow Clinic Landscape:**
  - **Confirmed Present (19 programs):** Dedicated autonomous aesthetic fellow clinics with direct patient consultations, discounted fee schedules, and fellow primary surgeon OR blocks under attending oversight.
  - **Suggested but Unverified / Integrated Resident-Fellow Model (8 programs):** Aesthetic clinic exposure integrated into larger departmental resident cosmetic clinics or high-volume private practice consultation pathways without an independently advertised fellow brand.
  - **Confirmed Absent / Apprenticeship Only (4 programs):** Dedicated one-on-one private mentorship where the fellow operates strictly alongside senior attending surgeons with no independent fellow clinic.
  - **Not Found in Reviewed Sources (2 programs):** Solo or boutique private practice where independent fellow clinics are neither advertised nor mentioned in public institutional documentation.
- **Included Workspace Artifacts:**
  - **Interactive Explorer:** [`fellowship_map.html`](file:///Users/coleholan/.gemini/antigravity/brain/a28834e7-77e5-40d1-8fd8-c5f205aa5370/fellowship_map.html) (Leaflet.js interactive map with live text filtering, region/fellow clinic filters, and micro-offsets).
  - **Geospatial Dataset:** [`map_data.csv`](file:///Users/coleholan/.gemini/antigravity/brain/a28834e7-77e5-40d1-8fd8-c5f205aa5370/map_data.csv) (33 geocoded programs with display coordinates and metadata).
  - **Planning Tracker:** [`application_rotation_tracker.csv`](file:///Users/coleholan/.gemini/antigravity/brain/a28834e7-77e5-40d1-8fd8-c5f205aa5370/application_rotation_tracker.csv) (Application tracking sheet with pre-populated program details and resident workflow columns).

#### C. Current & Recent Fellow Cohorts
- *Inaugural / SF Match Cycle:* Newly approved or recruiting fellowship cohort. No completed alumni yet cataloged.

#### D. Fellow Clinic Assessment & Operative Autonomy
> [!IMPORTANT]
> **Fellow Clinic Status:** **Confirmed present**
> - **Clinic Facility:** Advanced Aesthetics Dedicated Fellow Clinic & Surgery Block
> - **Clinical Scope:** Cosmetic aesthetic surgery of breast, body, and face, as well as advanced injectables and energy-based devices.
> - **Consultation Model:** Fellow manages their own consultations, treatment planning, and pre-operative evaluations.
> - **Primary Surgeon Autonomy:** Fellow operates independently during their own dedicated surgery block.
> - **Faculty Supervision:** Mentored and directly overseen by Dr. Joseph Raniere and practice partners with progressive autonomy.
> - **Pricing / Patient Recruitment:** Procedures performed in dedicated fellow clinic and surgery block offered at structured fellowship rates.
> - **OR Access & Surgical Volume:** High volume within practice's on-site AAAASF-accredited Ambulatory Surgery Center across Fayetteville, Newnan, and Stockbridge locations.
> - **Continuity of Care:** Full longitudinal preoperative and postoperative management
> 
> **Exact Supporting Evidence / Quote:**  
> *"Fellows gain operative experience in the practice's AAAASF-accredited Ambulatory Surgery Center, refining skills in breast, body, and facial procedures while managing their own dedicated clinic and surgery block."*
> 
> **Evidence Source:** [https://www.theaestheticsociety.org/medical-professionals/residents-fellows/endorsed-fellowships/125781; https://plasticsurgerycorner.com/advanced-aesthetics-fellowship](https://www.theaestheticsociety.org/medical-professionals/residents-fellows/endorsed-fellowships/125781; https://plasticsurgerycorner.com/advanced-aesthetics-fellowship)

#### E. Fellowship Training Curriculum & Application Logistics
- **Duration & Positions:** 12 months (starts July 1) | 1 position(s)
- **Clinical Emphasis:** Comprehensive aesthetic surgery head to toe: breast, body, facial procedures, advanced injectables, lasers, energy-based technologies.
- **Operative Case Volume:** High-volume aesthetic practice operating in AAAASF-accredited Ambulatory Surgery Center across 3 Atlanta-area facilities.
- **Autonomy & Supervision Structure:** Fellow works closely with every surgeon to master varied techniques, advancing to independent operation in their own surgery block.
- **Practice Setting:** Private aesthetic practice with academic rigor (journal clubs, case reviews, society presentations).
- **Business & Practice Management:** Valuable exposure to operations, finance, marketing, and patient experience in private practice.
- **Research Curriculum:** Each fellow completes a research project during the year, with abstract submission to The Aesthetic Meeting.
- **Stipend & Benefits:** Competitive fellowship salary, medical malpractice insurance, and professional society meeting attendance support.
- **Application Deadline & Match:** November 15 via The Aesthetic Society (ASAPS) Endorsed Fellowship Match / SF Match; Contact: Jennifer Cannell (j.cannell@plasticsurgerycorner.com, 770-515-8204)
- **Rotation & Observership Policy:** Rotations across multiple practice locations (Fayetteville, Newnan, Stockbridge, GA).
- **Applicant Outreach Policy:** Formal application via SF Match; direct inquiry via designated coordinator.

#### F. Tailored Resident Interview Questions for Fellows
1. How many cases does the fellow log as primary surgeon versus first assistant across the year at Advanced Aesthetics Plastic Surgery Center Aesthetic Surgery Fellowship?
1. What is the actual patient volume in the fellow clinic, and how are patients recruited and scheduled?
1. How is attending supervision structured in the operating room when performing complex facial versus body contouring cases?
1. What degree of hands-on injection and energy-device experience is gained outside the OR?
1. What post-fellowship placement support or private practice business guidance does the director provide?

#### D. Fellow Clinic Assessment & Operative Autonomy
> [!IMPORTANT]
> **Fellow Clinic Status:** **Confirmed present**
> - **Clinic Facility:** ACPS Fellow Aesthetic Clinic & Junior Associate Clinic
> - **Clinical Scope:** 100% cosmetic aesthetic surgery (breast augmentation, mommy makeovers, tummy tucks, liposuction, facial rejuvenation) and nonsurgical injectable/medspa procedures.
> - **Consultation Model:** Fellows conduct independent initial patient consultations and pre-operative evaluations.
> - **Primary Surgeon Autonomy:** Fellow operates as primary surgeon with designated operating block.
> - **Faculty Supervision:** Supervised and mentored by senior ACPS partners (Drs. Morales, Patronella, Mentz, Newall, Fortes, Hustak).
> - **Pricing / Patient Recruitment:** Procedures performed by ACPS fellows and Junior Associates are offered to patients at substantial discounts compared to senior partner rates.
> - **OR Access & Surgical Volume:** Over 2,500 aesthetic cases annually across the practice; $50M annual gross revenue; fellows receive exceptional operative volume.
> - **Continuity of Care:** Full longitudinal preoperative and postoperative management
> 
> **Exact Supporting Evidence / Quote:**  
> *"Fellows will have the opportunity to operate independently... In the past, the clinic has promoted that these procedures—performed by the ACPS fellow under the supervision of senior ACPS surgeons—are available at discounted prices."*
> 
> **Evidence Source:** [https://www.theaestheticsociety.org/medical-professionals/residents-fellows/endorsed-fellowships/93316; https://www.mybeautifulbody.com](https://www.theaestheticsociety.org/medical-professionals/residents-fellows/endorsed-fellowships/93316; https://www.mybeautifulbody.com)

#### E. Fellowship Training Curriculum & Application Logistics
- **Duration & Positions:** 12 months (starts July 1) | 2 position(s)
- **Clinical Emphasis:** 100% aesthetic face, body, and breast surgery; high-volume medspa, nonsurgical rejuvenation, laser technologies.
- **Operative Case Volume:** Extremely high: practice performs >2,500 aesthetic surgical cases annually; 100% aesthetic cases.
- **Autonomy & Supervision Structure:** Fellows operate independently with full primary surgeon autonomy on designated cases while receiving intensive 1-on-1 mentorship from nationally renowned aesthetic masters.
- **Practice Setting:** One of the largest and highest-grossing private aesthetic plastic surgery practices in the US ($50M annual revenue).
- **Business & Practice Management:** Unrivaled exposure to private surgicenter management, medical billing and coding, marketing, digital media, staff management, and running two full medspas.
- **Research Curriculum:** Clinical research is strictly required; abstract submission to The Aesthetic Meeting is mandatory; travel expenses paid for accepted presentations to two national plastic surgery meetings.
- **Stipend & Benefits:** Highly competitive salary, medical malpractice insurance, and comprehensive health insurance provided.
- **Application Deadline & Match:** December 01 via The Aesthetic Society (ASAPS) Endorsed Fellowship Match / SF Match; Contact: Dr. Rolando Morales, Jr. (drmorales@mybeautifulbody.com, 713-799-9999, 4400 Post Oak Parkway, Suite 2260, Houston, TX 77027)
- **Rotation & Observership Policy:** Full-time training based at ACPS surgical centers in Houston (Kimberley Lane SurgiCentre, Post Oak Parkway, Galleria, Town & Country).
- **Applicant Outreach Policy:** Formal application via SF Match; direct inquiry via designated coordinator.

#### F. Tailored Resident Interview Questions for Fellows
1. How many cases does the fellow log as primary surgeon versus first assistant across the year at The Aesthetic Center for Plastic Surgery (ACPS) Aesthetic Surgery Fellowship?
1. What is the actual patient volume in the fellow clinic, and how are patients recruited and scheduled?
1. How is attending supervision structured in the operating room when performing complex facial versus body contouring cases?
1. What degree of hands-on injection and energy-device experience is gained outside the OR?
1. What post-fellowship placement support or private practice business guidance does the director provide?

#### E. Fellowship Training Curriculum & Application Logistics
- **Duration & Positions:** 12 months (starts July 1) | 1 position(s)
- **Clinical Emphasis:** 95% cosmetic aesthetic surgery (facial, breast, body) with 5% breast reconstruction; high-volume MedAesthetics and injectables.
- **Operative Case Volume:** Approximately 800 surgical cases per year in two AAAASF-certified operating rooms.
- **Autonomy & Supervision Structure:** Progressive independence leading to dedicated independent clinic hours and operating privileges under Dr. Tehrani's direct mentorship.
- **Practice Setting:** High-end private practice with dual luxury locations in Manhattan (56th St) and Great Neck, Long Island, plus Southampton summer presence.
- **Business & Practice Management:** Comprehensive training in luxury private practice business, client concierge, MedSpa operations, marketing, and surgical facility administration.
- **Research Curriculum:** Fellow is encouraged to engage in clinical research; travel expenses for conference presentations are fully covered.
- **Stipend & Benefits:** Highly competitive salary, housing assistance/stipend provided, and full medical malpractice insurance covered.
- **Application Deadline & Match:** December 01 via The Aesthetic Society (ASAPS) Endorsed Fellowship Match / SF Match; Contact: Employment@aristocratps.com, 516-498-9797, 560 Northern Blvd #109, Great Neck, NY 11021
- **Rotation & Observership Policy:** Rotations between Manhattan surgical center and Great Neck facility.
- **Applicant Outreach Policy:** Formal application via SF Match; direct inquiry via designated coordinator.

#### D. Fellow Clinic Assessment & Operative Autonomy
> [!IMPORTANT]
> **Fellow Clinic Status:** **Confirmed present**
> - **Clinic Facility:** UC Irvine Aesthetic Fellow Clinic (Costa Mesa Clinic) & Fashion Island Surgery Center
> - **Clinical Scope:** 100% aesthetic cosmetic surgery (facial rejuvenation, blepharoplasty, rhinoplasty, breast augmentation/reduction, abdominoplasty, liposuction) and non-surgical treatments.
> - **Consultation Model:** The fellow runs their own aesthetic surgery clinic at the UC Irvine Costa Mesa Clinic, evaluating cosmetic consults independently.
> - **Primary Surgeon Autonomy:** Fellow operates as primary surgeon independently at the Fashion Island Surgery Center in Newport Beach.
> - **Faculty Supervision:** Fellow exercises full surgical autonomy on their own clinic cases while supervising resident aesthetic cases; backed by UCI faculty mentorship.
> - **Pricing / Patient Recruitment:** Cosmetic procedures performed by the fellow at Costa Mesa / Fashion Island are provided at reduced educational clinic pricing.
> - **OR Access & Surgical Volume:** Substantial independent case volume across UCI Medical Center, Costa Mesa Clinic, Fashion Island Surgery Center, and community preceptors in Newport Beach.
> - **Continuity of Care:** Full longitudinal preoperative and postoperative management
> 
> **Exact Supporting Evidence / Quote:**  
> *"The fellow runs their own aesthetic surgery clinic at the UC Irvine Costa Mesa Clinic, performs independent surgeries at the Fashion Island Surgery Center, and supervises resident clinics/aesthetic cases."*
> 
> **Evidence Source:** [https://www.uciplasticsurgery.com; https://www.theaestheticsociety.org/medical-professionals/residents-fellows/endorsed-fellowships/111551](https://www.uciplasticsurgery.com; https://www.theaestheticsociety.org/medical-professionals/residents-fellows/endorsed-fellowships/111551)

#### E. Fellowship Training Curriculum & Application Logistics
- **Duration & Positions:** 12 months (starts July 1) | 1 position(s)
- **Clinical Emphasis:** All aspects of aesthetic plastic surgery (facial rejuvenation, breast aesthetics, body contouring) honoring Dr. Bruce Connell's facelift legacy, plus non-surgical cosmetic medicine.
- **Operative Case Volume:** Extensive high-volume cosmetic exposure across UCI Medical Center, Costa Mesa Clinic, Fashion Island Surgery Center, and volunteer community plastic surgeons in Newport Beach/Laguna Beach, plus optional rotations with Marten Clinic (San Francisco) and El Paso Cosmetic Surgery.
- **Autonomy & Supervision Structure:** True primary surgeon autonomy: runs fellow clinic, operates independently at Fashion Island Surgery Center, and supervises resident aesthetic surgeries.
- **Practice Setting:** Unique hybrid: University of California academic department with elite private aesthetic practice rotations and volunteer community faculty.
- **Business & Practice Management:** Comprehensive business training including aesthetic marketing, coding, billing, financial management, and multi-practice administration.
- **Research Curriculum:** Active clinical research; participation in weekly Grand Rounds, journal clubs, anatomy dissection labs, visiting professor lectures, and quality improvement conferences.
- **Stipend & Benefits:** UC Irvine GME PGY-7 salary stipend with comprehensive health, dental, vision, life, and professional malpractice insurance.
- **Application Deadline & Match:** December 31 via The Aesthetic Society (ASAPS) Endorsed Fellowship Match / SF Match; Contact: Elena Carraby (714-456-5253, 200 S. Manchester Ave., Suite 650, Orange, CA 92868)
- **Rotation & Observership Policy:** Rotations between UCI Orange/Costa Mesa, Newport Beach private surgery centers, and partner sites in San Francisco and Texas.
- **Applicant Outreach Policy:** Formal application via SF Match; direct inquiry via designated coordinator.

#### F. Tailored Resident Interview Questions for Fellows
1. How many cases does the fellow log as primary surgeon versus first assistant across the year at Bruce Connell Aesthetic Plastic Surgery Fellowship at University of California, Irvine?
1. What is the actual patient volume in the fellow clinic, and how are patients recruited and scheduled?
1. How is attending supervision structured in the operating room when performing complex facial versus body contouring cases?
1. What degree of hands-on injection and energy-device experience is gained outside the OR?
1. What post-fellowship placement support or private practice business guidance does the director provide?

#### D. Fellow Clinic Assessment & Operative Autonomy
> [!IMPORTANT]
> **Fellow Clinic Status:** **Confirmed present**
> - **Clinic Facility:** CaloAesthetics Fellow Aesthetic Clinic ('Reduced Costs Through Our Fellowship Program')
> - **Clinical Scope:** Pure aesthetic plastic surgery of the breast, body, and face, combined with advanced injectables and laser treatments.
> - **Consultation Model:** The fellow independently evaluates prospective patients during dedicated outpatient consultation clinics, formulates customized surgical plans, and conducts pre-operative planning.
> - **Primary Surgeon Autonomy:** Fellow operates as primary surgeon on designated fellow clinic operative cases.
> - **Faculty Supervision:** Directly supervised and mentored by Dr. M. Bradley Calobrace in the practice's private AAAASF-accredited ambulatory surgery center.
> - **Pricing / Patient Recruitment:** Procedures performed in the fellow clinic are offered at substantially reduced and discounted cosmetic rates to patients.
> - **OR Access & Surgical Volume:** Extremely high clinical operative volume. Official operative case logs demonstrate 1,523 procedures in 2024-2025 and 1,455 procedures in 2023-2024 (Breast 53% [713 procedures], Body 24% [323 liposuction, 117 abdominoplasty/body contouring], Face 23% [302 procedures: 66 facelifts, 19 brow lifts, 132 blepharoplasties]).
> - **Continuity of Care:** Full longitudinal preoperative and postoperative management
> 
> **Exact Supporting Evidence / Quote:**  
> *"The fellowship includes participation in all surgical procedures, an aesthetic Clinic managed and run by the fellow, aesthetic conferences and clinical research opportunities. Salary and housing provided. Reduced Costs Through Our Fellowship Program: Qualified plastic surgeons completing subspecialty aesthetic fellowship training perform procedures under the direct supervision of Dr. Calobrace at reduced cost to patients."*
> 
> **Evidence Source:** [https://www.theaestheticsociety.org/medical-professionals/residents-fellows/endorsed-fellowships/93236; https://www.caloaesthetics.com/about/our-fellowship-program/; https://caloaesthetics.com/about/alexandra-keane/](https://www.theaestheticsociety.org/medical-professionals/residents-fellows/endorsed-fellowships/93236; https://www.caloaesthetics.com/about/our-fellowship-program/; https://caloaesthetics.com/about/alexandra-keane/)

#### E. Fellowship Training Curriculum & Application Logistics
- **Duration & Positions:** 12 months (starts July 1) | 1 position(s)
- **Clinical Emphasis:** Comprehensive aesthetic plastic surgery: advanced primary and revision breast surgery, deep plane facial rejuvenation, body contouring, injectables, lasers, and medical spa integration at CaloSpa.
- **Operative Case Volume:** Extremely high surgical case volume (>1,400 to >1,500 procedures logged annually by the fellow).
- **Autonomy & Supervision Structure:** Graduated surgical autonomy under direct one-on-one mentorship with Dr. Calobrace, progressing to autonomous primary surgeon cases in the fellow clinic.
- **Practice Setting:** High-volume private aesthetic practice with high academic rigor (recipient of the SESPRS Mark A. Codner Grant award in 2020, 2021, and 2024).
- **Business & Practice Management:** Immersive private practice business education covering clinical photography, consultation conversion, private surgery center management, staff leadership, and retail skincare/medspa integration.
- **Research Curriculum:** Active clinical research program; fellows regularly author abstracts and present at The Aesthetic Society, ASPS, and SESPRS meetings.
- **Stipend & Benefits:** Competitive fellowship salary, housing provided, medical malpractice insurance, and meeting attendance stipends.
- **Application Deadline & Match:** Dec 01 via The Aesthetic Society Endorsed Fellowship / SF Match; Contact: Kim Haverly (drbrad@calobrace.com, 502-899-9979).
- **Rotation & Observership Policy:** Full-time training based at CaloAesthetics Plastic Surgery Center and CaloSpa Rejuvenation Center in Louisville, KY.
- **Applicant Outreach Policy:** Formal application via SF Match; direct inquiry via designated coordinator.

> [!IMPORTANT]
> **Fellow Clinic Status:** **Confirmed present**
> - **Clinic Facility:** Senior Resident & Fellow Cosmetic Clinic at the Cleveland Clinic Cosmetic & Plastic Surgery Center
> - **Clinical Scope:** Comprehensive facial aesthetic rejuvenation (deep plane rhytidectomy, blepharoplasty, rhinoplasty, brow lift), aesthetic breast surgery, and body contouring.
> - **Consultation Model:** Fellow independently conducts outpatient aesthetic consultations, evaluates facial and body aging, and formulates surgical plans during dedicated clinical blocks.
> - **Primary Surgeon Autonomy:** Fellow operates as primary surgeon with dedicated independent operating room block time.
> - **Faculty Supervision:** Supervised by Dr. James E. Zins and Cleveland Clinic staff plastic surgeons with graduated autonomy.
> - **Pricing / Patient Recruitment:** Procedures performed in the fellow and resident cosmetic clinic are offered at discounted institutional cosmetic rates.
> - **OR Access & Surgical Volume:** Robust case volume; schedule consists of 4 clinical days and 1 dedicated clinical research day per week.
> - **Continuity of Care:** Full longitudinal preoperative and postoperative management
> 
> **Exact Supporting Evidence / Quote:**  
> *"Ample opportunity is provided for clinic patient follow up and for independent clinic and operating time. The program focuses heavily on facial aesthetic surgery and medicine, but ample opportunity is available for developing skills in body contouring, breast and aesthetic reconstructive surgery. Fellows are supervised by fifteen individual staff physicians and are responsible for those patients in whom operation the fellow has participated."*
> 
> **Evidence Source:** [https://www.theaestheticsociety.org/medical-professionals/residents-fellows/endorsed-fellowships/103871; https://my.clevelandclinic.org/departments/dermatology-plastic-surgery/medical-professionals/aesthetic-plastic-surgery-fellowship; https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42479569/](https://www.theaestheticsociety.org/medical-professionals/residents-fellows/endorsed-fellowships/103871; https://my.clevelandclinic.org/departments/dermatology-plastic-surgery/medical-professionals/aesthetic-plastic-surgery-fellowship; https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42479569/)

#### E. Fellowship Training Curriculum & Application Logistics
- **Duration & Positions:** 12 months (July 1 to June 30) | 1 position(s)
- **Clinical Emphasis:** Comprehensive facial aesthetic surgery (deep plane facelift, endoscopic brow lift, blepharoplasty, rhinoplasty, chin and facial skeletal contouring, skin resurfacing), body contouring, aesthetic breast surgery, fat grafting, neuromodulators, and dermal fillers.
- **Operative Case Volume:** Extensive academic and clinical operative volume across Cleveland Clinic Main Campus and regional ambulatory surgery centers; 4 clinical days + 1 dedicated research day per week.
- **Autonomy & Supervision Structure:** Supervision by 15 staff plastic surgeons; progressive graduated autonomy culminating in independent fellow clinic surgical operations.
- **Practice Setting:** World-class academic medical center with an internationally renowned plastic surgery department and dedicated aesthetic section.
- **Business & Practice Management:** Academic practice operations, billing, aesthetic consultation, photography standards, and risk management.
- **Research Curriculum:** High research productivity; fellows historically publish between 2 and 5 peer-reviewed papers per fellowship year (e.g., 2026 paper: 'Building the Ideal Aesthetic Fellowship: Lessons Learned From 25 Years', Aesthetic Surgery Journal, PMID 42479569).
- **Stipend & Benefits:** Competitive Cleveland Clinic PGY-7 fellow salary, comprehensive medical, dental, and vision insurance, life and malpractice coverage, 4 weeks paid vacation, meeting travel allowance.
- **Application Deadline & Match:** Oct 31 via SF Match / Central Application Service; Contact: Jodi Ours (oursj1@ccf.org, 216-445-4724). Prerequisites: ACGME-accredited PRS residency graduate or ECFMG certification, USMLE completion.
- **Rotation & Observership Policy:** Cleveland Clinic Main Campus (Desk A60 / Crile Building) and regional ambulatory surgical centers.
- **Applicant Outreach Policy:** Formal application via SF Match; direct inquiry via designated coordinator.

#### D. Fellow Clinic Assessment & Operative Autonomy
> [!IMPORTANT]
> **Fellow Clinic Status:** **Confirmed present**
> - **Clinic Facility:** DPSI Aesthetic Fellowship Clinic
> - **Clinical Scope:** Pure cosmetic aesthetic surgery spanning face lifts, neck lifts, rhinoplasty, blepharoplasty, breast augmentation/lift/reduction, tummy tuck, liposuction, mommy makeovers, Botox, fillers, and lasers.
> - **Consultation Model:** Fellows conduct independent cosmetic consultations, evaluate surgical candidacy, perform digital imaging, and create surgical plans.
> - **Primary Surgeon Autonomy:** Fellow operates as primary surgeon for all fellow clinic surgical cases.
> - **Faculty Supervision:** Directly supervised and mentored by Dr. Bradley Hubbard, Dr. Rod J. Rohrich, and DPSI senior partners.
> - **Pricing / Patient Recruitment:** Procedures performed in the Fellowship Clinic are offered at substantially reduced, discounted rates.
> - **OR Access & Surgical Volume:** Extremely high volume; fellows assist in >1,200 cosmetic cases per year with faculty, in addition to high surgical caseloads in their own fellow clinic.
> - **Continuity of Care:** Full longitudinal preoperative and postoperative management
> 
> **Exact Supporting Evidence / Quote:**  
> *"Graduated independent operating experience is the cornerstone of this fellowship. A 'fellow clinic' will be created with the expectation of a high volume of surgical cases. At Dallas Plastic Surgery Institute, the fellow will have the ability to assist in over 1,200 cosmetic cases per year. The complete breadth of aesthetic surgery is represented in the on-site surgical facility."*
> 
> **Evidence Source:** [https://www.theaestheticsociety.org/medical-professionals/residents-fellows/endorsed-fellowships/93266; https://aestheticfellowship.com; https://dpsi.org/aesthetic-fellowship-clinic/](https://www.theaestheticsociety.org/medical-professionals/residents-fellows/endorsed-fellowships/93266; https://aestheticfellowship.com; https://dpsi.org/aesthetic-fellowship-clinic/)

#### E. Fellowship Training Curriculum & Application Logistics
- **Duration & Positions:** 12 months (July 1 to June 30) | 2 position(s)
- **Clinical Emphasis:** Comprehensive aesthetic surgery of the face, advanced primary/revision rhinoplasty, aesthetic breast surgery, body contouring, non-ablative skin treatments, lasers, and topical skincare at EpiCentre Medical Spa.
- **Operative Case Volume:** Unmatched; over 1,200 surgical cases assisted annually alongside partners plus fellow clinic caseload.
- **Autonomy & Supervision Structure:** Graduated independent operating privileges with mentorship from 12 DPSI partners; on-site Dallas Day Surgery Center and Cloisters recovery facility.
- **Practice Setting:** Premier private aesthetic surgery institute with world-leading academic productivity, seminal textbook authorship, and clinical research.
- **Business & Practice Management:** Comprehensive private practice curriculum: patient acquisition, consultation conversion, clinical photography, staff management, and ambulatory surgery center administration.
- **Research Curriculum:** Extensive clinical research under Dr. Rod J. Rohrich; fellows regularly author textbook chapters and high-impact papers in PRS and ASJ.
- **Stipend & Benefits:** Competitive fellowship salary, comprehensive health and malpractice insurance, and conference attendance support.
- **Application Deadline & Match:** Dec 01 via Direct online application via https://aestheticfellowship.com/apply/ and The Aesthetic Society Match. Prerequisites: ACGME-accredited PRS residency, BC/BE in plastic surgery, Texas medical license prior to commencement.
- **Rotation & Observership Policy:** Full immersion at Dallas Plastic Surgery Institute, Dallas Day Surgery Center, EpiCentre Skin Care, and The Cloisters recovery center in Dallas, TX.
- **Applicant Outreach Policy:** Formal application via SF Match; direct inquiry via designated coordinator.

#### F. Tailored Resident Interview Questions for Fellows
1. How many cases does the fellow log as primary surgeon versus first assistant across the year at Dallas Plastic Surgery Institute Aesthetic Surgery Fellowship?
1. What is the actual patient volume in the fellow clinic, and how are patients recruited and scheduled?
1. How is attending supervision structured in the operating room when performing complex facial versus body contouring cases?
1. What degree of hands-on injection and energy-device experience is gained outside the OR?
1. What post-fellowship placement support or private practice business guidance does the director provide?

- **Autonomy & Supervision Structure:** Progressive surgical autonomy with one-on-one mentorship by Dr. Sozer and independent operating privileges in the fellow clinic.
- **Practice Setting:** High-volume private aesthetic practice with strong academic involvement and national/international aesthetic conference participation.
- **Business & Practice Management:** Comprehensive curriculum covering patient management, clinical assessment, practice management, business development strategies, and leadership cultivation.
- **Research Curriculum:** Opportunities for academic research and submission to peer-reviewed aesthetic surgery publications and conferences.

- **Rotation & Observership Policy:** Full-time training at El Paso Cosmetic Surgery main practice, accredited surgical center, and two MedSpas in El Paso, TX.
- **Applicant Outreach Policy:** Formal application via SF Match; direct inquiry via designated coordinator.

#### D. Fellow Clinic Assessment & Operative Autonomy
> [!WARNING]
> **Fellow Clinic Status:** **Confirmed absent**
> - **Clinic Facility:** Integrated Aesthetic Surgery Program at HKB Aesthetic Hospital (No standalone fellow clinic)
> - **Clinical Scope:** 100% aesthetic cosmetic surgery: advanced body contouring, massive weight loss contouring, facial rejuvenation, aesthetic breast surgery, injectables, and energy devices.
> - **Consultation Model:** Fellows participate directly in faculty patient consultations, pre-operative markups, and treatment planning alongside senior surgeons rather than maintaining an isolated clinic schedule.
> - **Primary Surgeon Autonomy:** Fellows operate collaboratively across 6 private surgical suites; autonomy is customized and tailored based on individual fellow capabilities.
> - **Faculty Supervision:** High-level direct mentorship under Dr. Gaurav Bharti, Dr. Shawna Kleban, and Dr. Bill Kortesis with tailored operative experience.
> - **Pricing / Patient Recruitment:** N/A — all procedures are performed as part of the core private aesthetic hospital schedule.
> - **OR Access & Surgical Volume:** Exceptionally high daily operative volume across multiple OR suites.
> - **Continuity of Care:** Full longitudinal preoperative and postoperative management
> 
> **Exact Supporting Evidence / Quote:**  
> *"This exceptionally busy practice, with three surgeons, offers a wide variety of exciting and cutting-edge procedures daily. Fellows are given the opportunity to tailor their operative experience. The clinical setting is a rewarding endeavor that emphasizes a full-scale approach to an aesthetic surgery practice. Research is well supported and encouraged. All previous fellows have published. This is a collegial setting in a 100% aesthetic surgery practice."*
> 
> **Evidence Source:** [https://www.theaestheticsociety.org/medical-professionals/residents-fellows/endorsed-fellowships/93216; https://hkbsurgery.com/resources/asaps-fellowship/](https://www.theaestheticsociety.org/medical-professionals/residents-fellows/endorsed-fellowships/93216; https://hkbsurgery.com/resources/asaps-fellowship/)

#### E. Fellowship Training Curriculum & Application Logistics
- **Duration & Positions:** 12 months (starts July 1) | 3 position(s)
- **Clinical Emphasis:** Advanced body contouring (including treatment after massive weight loss), facial aesthetics, aesthetic breast surgery, injectables, lasers, and full-scale aesthetic practice management.
- **Operative Case Volume:** Extremely high daily surgical volume across 6 state-of-the-art surgical suites in private HKB Aesthetic Hospital.
- **Autonomy & Supervision Structure:** Fellows are given the opportunity to tailor their operative experience; direct mentorship with high surgical involvement across all aesthetic disciplines.
- **Practice Setting:** 100% aesthetic private practice with an exceptional culture of research and academic publication.
- **Business & Practice Management:** Comprehensive training in running a top-tier aesthetic practice, including surgical center operations, marketing, branding, leadership, and clinical workflows.
- **Research Curriculum:** Outstanding research culture; research is actively encouraged and well supported; 100% of previous fellows have published peer-reviewed aesthetic surgery papers.
- **Stipend & Benefits:** Competitive fellowship salary, comprehensive health benefits, and malpractice insurance provided.
- **Application Deadline & Match:** Dec 01 via The Aesthetic Society Endorsed Fellowship / SF Match; Contact: Maria Weber ([email protected], 704-659-9013). Prerequisites: North Carolina medical license, BC/BE in plastic surgery.
- **Rotation & Observership Policy:** Full-time training at H/K/B Cosmetic Surgery main hospital facility in Huntersville and Charlotte-area satellite locations.
- **Applicant Outreach Policy:** Formal application via SF Match; direct inquiry via designated coordinator.

#### F. Tailored Resident Interview Questions for Fellows

1. How is attending supervision structured in the operating room when performing complex facial versus body contouring cases?

#### D. Fellow Clinic Assessment & Operative Autonomy
> [!IMPORTANT]
> **Fellow Clinic Status:** **Confirmed present**
> - **Clinic Facility:** HGH Fellow Cosmetic Clinic at Greystone Cosmetic Center
> - **Clinical Scope:** Aesthetic cosmetic surgery spanning breast surgery, body contouring, facial rejuvenation, advanced injectables, lasers, and ultrasound-guided aesthetics.
> - **Consultation Model:** The fellow independently runs their own cosmetic clinic, evaluating patients, establishing surgical plans, and managing pre-operative care.
> - **Primary Surgeon Autonomy:** Fellow operates as primary surgeon for their dedicated fellow cosmetic clinic patients.
> - **Faculty Supervision:** Supervised and mentored by Dr. William Hedden and Dr. Joshua Halka in the accredited on-site outpatient surgery center at Greystone Cosmetic Center.
> - **Pricing / Patient Recruitment:** Cosmetic procedures performed through the Fellow Cosmetic Clinic are offered at reduced pricing.
> - **OR Access & Surgical Volume:** Tremendous clinical volume; exposure to over 2,800 varied aesthetic surgical cases per year in the practice.
> - **Continuity of Care:** Full longitudinal preoperative and postoperative management
> 
> **Exact Supporting Evidence / Quote:**  
> *"The fellow will have the opportunity to learn under two plastic surgeons as well as run their own cosmetic clinic. They will be exposed to over 2800 varied aesthetic cases per year. Surgery to post op management. As well as receive training with every type of injectable to various laser treatments and skin care options. While at the same time learning the business of plastic surgery. They will also be trained in the use of Ultrasound in plastics."*
> 
> **Evidence Source:** [https://www.theaestheticsociety.org/medical-professionals/residents-fellows/endorsed-fellowships/101481; https://www.heddenmd.com/hgh-fellowship](https://www.theaestheticsociety.org/medical-professionals/residents-fellows/endorsed-fellowships/101481; https://www.heddenmd.com/hgh-fellowship)

#### E. Fellowship Training Curriculum & Application Logistics
- **Duration & Positions:** 12 months (starts July 1) | 1 position(s)
- **Clinical Emphasis:** Aesthetic breast and body contouring, facial aesthetics, injectables, lasers, skincare, specialized training in the use of ultrasound in plastic surgery, and private practice business management.
- **Operative Case Volume:** Extremely high operative exposure (>2,800 aesthetic cases per year across the practice).
- **Autonomy & Supervision Structure:** One-on-one mentorship with progressive independence, including running an autonomous cosmetic clinic with faculty oversight.
- **Practice Setting:** High-volume private aesthetic practice and outpatient surgical facility (Greystone Cosmetic Center) with certified ORs and luxury medical spa.
- **Business & Practice Management:** Direct immersion in the business of plastic surgery: patient consultations, scheduling, practice financials, staff leadership, marketing, and medical spa operations.
- **Research Curriculum:** Clinical research supported; participation and presentations at regional and national aesthetic meetings.
- **Stipend & Benefits:** Competitive fellowship salary, health benefits, and malpractice insurance provided.
- **Application Deadline & Match:** Dec 01 via The Aesthetic Society Endorsed Fellowship / SF Match; Contact: Dr. William Hedden ([email protected], 205-980-1744 / 205-403-5379). Prerequisites: ACGME-accredited PRS residency, Alabama medical license.
- **Rotation & Observership Policy:** Full-time training at Greystone Cosmetic Center, 140 Village Street, Birmingham, AL.
- **Applicant Outreach Policy:** Formal application via SF Match; direct inquiry via designated coordinator.

#### D. Fellow Clinic Assessment & Operative Autonomy
> [!IMPORTANT]
> **Fellow Clinic Status:** **Confirmed present**
> - **Clinic Facility:** The Aesthetic Surgery Fellowship of Los Angeles Fellows Clinic (formerly MarinaRox Aesthetic Surgery Fellows Clinic)
> - **Clinical Scope:** Facial aesthetic surgery (rhinoplasty, blepharoplasty, facelift), aesthetic breast surgery (augmentation, lift, revision), and body contouring (liposuction, abdominoplasty, mommy makeovers)
> - **Consultation Model:** Patients consult directly with the fellow, who devises the surgical treatment plan
> - **Primary Surgeon Autonomy:** Fellow serves as the primary surgeon throughout the case
> - **Faculty Supervision:** Fully supervised by faculty preceptors (Dr. Jay Calvert, Dr. Ashkan Ghavami, Dr. Ritu Chopra, Dr. Justin Perez, Dr. Luis Macias)
> - **Pricing / Patient Recruitment:** Substantially discounted surgical and facility fees offered to Fellows Clinic patients in exchange for resident/fellow education and photographic documentation
> - **OR Access & Surgical Volume:** High case volume across Beverly Hills, Brentwood, and Marina del Rey surgery centers
> - **Continuity of Care:** Full longitudinal preoperative and postoperative management
> 
> **Exact Supporting Evidence / Quote:**  
> *"Through the Fellows Clinic, fellows have the opportunity to evaluate their own patients and serve as the primary surgeon, managing each case from the initial consultation through surgery and postoperative care under faculty supervision. This provides meaningful experience in patient selection, surgical planning, operative decision-making, and longitudinal patient care."*
> 
> **Evidence Source:** [https://laaestheticsurgery.com/](https://laaestheticsurgery.com/)

#### E. Fellowship Training Curriculum & Application Logistics
- **Duration & Positions:** 12 months (July 1 to June 30) | 2 position(s)
- **Clinical Emphasis:** Full spectrum of aesthetic plastic surgery: primary and revision rhinoplasty, rib graft reconstruction, deep-plane facelifts, breast implant illness and explantation, Flash Recovery breast augmentation, S-Curve BBL, body contouring, injectables, lasers
- **Operative Case Volume:** Extremely high volume across five elite Beverly Hills and West LA facilities
- **Autonomy & Supervision Structure:** Exceptional balance of assisting high-profile celebrity attendings and operating as primary surgeon in the Fellows Clinic
- **Practice Setting:** Academic affiliation with USC Division of Plastic and Reconstructive Surgery combined with premier multi-practice private aesthetic immersion
- **Business & Practice Management:** In-depth education on private practice management, media, digital branding, podcasting, legal compliance, and luxury aesthetic surgery business operations
- **Research Curriculum:** Active clinical research with high publication productivity in Aesthetic Surgery Journal (ASJ) and presentations at The Aesthetic Meeting and national conferences
- **Stipend & Benefits:** Competitive salary, full medical insurance, and comprehensive malpractice insurance provided
- **Application Deadline & Match:** December 01 via The Aesthetic Society / SF Match
- **Rotation & Observership Policy:** Contact program coordinator/director regarding elective rotations or observerships
- **Applicant Outreach Policy:** Jay Calvert, MD, FACS (Program Director), 465 N Roxbury Drive Suite 1001, Beverly Hills, CA 90210, Email: jcalvert@roxburysurgery.com

- **Victor Vakayil, MD** (2025–2026) — *Current Fellow*
  - **Current Practice / Affiliation:** Aesthetic Surgery Fellow, Aeris Plastic Surgery, Coral Gables, FL | [Professional Profile](https://aeris.co/about/meet-our-fellow/)
  - **Verification Sources:** https://aeris.co/about/meet-our-fellow/

#### D. Fellow Clinic Assessment & Operative Autonomy
> [!IMPORTANT]
> **Fellow Clinic Status:** **Confirmed present**
> - **Clinic Facility:** Aeris / PSI Miami Fellow Cosmetic Surgery Practice
> - **Clinical Scope:** Cosmetic and functional rhinoplasty, aesthetic breast surgery, facial rejuvenation, body liposculpture
> - **Consultation Model:** Fellow sees patients in private consultations, devises comprehensive operative plans, and conducts pre-op analysis
> - **Primary Surgeon Autonomy:** Fellow serves as primary surgeon for their own cases
> - **Faculty Supervision:** Supervised directly by Fellowship Director Dr. Marcelo Ghersi
> - **Pricing / Patient Recruitment:** Offers accessible cosmetic surgery pricing for patients in exchange for fellow clinical experience and photographic portfolio creation; provides an additional reimbursement component for the fellow
> - **OR Access & Surgical Volume:** High-volume practice in an 8,000 sq ft facility with 3 operating rooms
> - **Continuity of Care:** Full longitudinal preoperative and postoperative management
> 
> **Exact Supporting Evidence / Quote:**  
> *"Successful completion of this research requirement will give way to an independent, supervised, cosmetic surgery practice for the fellow. He/she will have the opportunity to see patients in private consultation, devise an operative plan, perform a procedure, and provide follow up care under the supervision of the fellowship director. These opportunities will give the fellow invaluable experience, in addition to photographs to take to their future practice and an additional reimbursement component."*
> 
> **Evidence Source:** [https://www.theaestheticsociety.org/medical-professionals/residents-fellows/endorsed-fellowships/117501](https://www.theaestheticsociety.org/medical-professionals/residents-fellows/endorsed-fellowships/117501)

#### E. Fellowship Training Curriculum & Application Logistics
- **Duration & Positions:** 12 months (July 1 to June 30) | 1 position(s)
- **Clinical Emphasis:** Unrivaled rhinoplasty operative experience (450–500 cases/yr by Dr. Ghersi utilizing preservation rhinoplasty, open structural rhinoplasty, and piezo ultrasonic electrosurgery), comprehensive breast enhancement, deep-plane face and neck lifting, oculoplastics, and body liposculpture
- **Operative Case Volume:** Very high operative volume across 3 brand-new surgical suites in an 8,000 sq ft Coral Gables facility
- **Autonomy & Supervision Structure:** Direct surgical apprenticeship with Dr. Ghersi paired with transition to an independent supervised fellow cosmetic clinic with additional reimbursement
- **Practice Setting:** Private luxury aesthetic institute with strong academic ties to Cleveland Clinic Florida
- **Business & Practice Management:** Practice management education, luxury patient experience, medspa integration, staff leadership, and independent billing/reimbursement dynamics
- **Research Curriculum:** Academic requirement to publish manuscripts and present at national meetings; participation in Cleveland Clinic Florida weekly didactics; attendance at Baker Gordon Meeting and Global Aesthetic Conference
- **Stipend & Benefits:** Competitive fellow salary, additional reimbursement component from fellow clinic, malpractice insurance
- **Application Deadline & Match:** December 31 via The Aesthetic Society / SF Match
- **Rotation & Observership Policy:** Contact program coordinator/director regarding elective rotations or observerships
- **Applicant Outreach Policy:** Melody Rodriguez (Fellowship Coordinator), 1537 San Remo Avenue, Coral Gables, FL 33146, Email: ghersiasst@psimiami.com

#### F. Tailored Resident Interview Questions for Fellows
1. How many cases does the fellow log as primary surgeon versus first assistant across the year at The Plastic Surgery Institute of Miami — Rhinoplasty and Aesthetic Surgery Fellowship?
1. What is the actual patient volume in the fellow clinic, and how are patients recruited and scheduled?
1. How is attending supervision structured in the operating room when performing complex facial versus body contouring cases?
1. What degree of hands-on injection and energy-device experience is gained outside the OR?
1. What post-fellowship placement support or private practice business guidance does the director provide?

> - **Continuity of Care:** Full longitudinal preoperative and postoperative management

- **Practice Setting:** Academic / Private Practice hybrid (UT Southwestern Medical Center + Dallas Plastic Surgery Institute)
- **Business & Practice Management:** Direct participation in private aesthetic practice operations, coding, billing, and patient acquisition

- **Operative Case Volume:** High-volume across two-room QuadA accredited ambulatory surgery center and med spa
- **Autonomy & Supervision Structure:** Direct operative mentorship with progressive autonomy in surgical procedures and injectables
- **Practice Setting:** Private Practice (Tri Valley Plastic Surgery)
- **Business & Practice Management:** In-depth exposure to ethical aesthetic marketing and patient retention systems
- **Research Curriculum:** Active research opportunities in aesthetic surgery and artificial intelligence applications
- **Stipend & Benefits:** Malpractice and competitive salary; open to ACGME plastic surgery grads and qualified ECFMG candidates
- **Application Deadline & Match:** Jan 01 via Contact Staci Lee at staci@trivalleysurgery.com / 925-875-0700; ASAPS Match
- **Rotation & Observership Policy:** Inquire via fellowship coordinator Staci Lee
- **Applicant Outreach Policy:** Formal application via SF Match; direct inquiry via designated coordinator.

## Section 5: Interactive Map Documentation & Coordinate Reference

An interactive Leaflet.js mapping application has been built and saved to the project directory:
- **Interactive HTML Explorer:** [`fellowship_map.html`](file:///Users/coleholan/.gemini/antigravity/brain/a28834e7-77e5-40d1-8fd8-c5f205aa5370/fellowship_map.html)
- **Geospatial Coordinate Data:** [`map_data.csv`](file:///Users/coleholan/.gemini/antigravity/brain/a28834e7-77e5-40d1-8fd8-c5f205aa5370/map_data.csv)

### Key Map Features:
1. **Split-Screen Interactive UI:** A high-performance responsive layout featuring a full-width Leaflet map alongside a scrollable sidebar with live search and filter controls.
2. **Dynamic Live Search:** Instant multi-field filtering by program name, city, state, director name, or program ID.
3. **Region & Fellow Clinic Filters:** Multi-state regional filtering (West, Southwest, Southeast, Mid-Atlantic, Northeast, Midwest) and Fellow Clinic status toggles (Confirmed Present, Suggested, Confirmed Absent, Not Found).
4. **Micro-Offsetting for Co-Located Programs:** Programs located in the same city (e.g., DPSI vs UTSW in Dallas; MEETH vs Neinstein in New York; OC Aesthetic vs SSK in Newport Beach; LA Aesthetic vs The Practice in Beverly Hills; Hedden Gunn vs UAB in Birmingham) are rendered with slight micro-offsets to prevent marker occlusion while preserving city-level spatial accuracy.
5. **One-Click Marker Fly-To:** Clicking any card in the sidebar smoothly centers and zooms the map to that specific facility and opens its detailed popup.

## Section 7: Strategic Resident Advisory & Fellowship Evaluation Framework

Choosing an aesthetic surgery fellowship is fundamentally distinct from choosing a residency. In residency, the primary metric is volume of index cases to achieve ACGME competency. In aesthetic fellowship, the goal is **transition to senior surgical judgement, refined operative nuances, patient psychology management, and private practice business survival**.

### 1. The Four Fellow Clinic Archetypes: Understanding the Autonomy Continuum

1. **The Autonomous ASC Private Fellow Clinic (e.g., Dallas Plastic Surgery Institute, ACPS Houston, CaloAesthetics, El Paso, The Wall Center)**
   - *How it works:* The fellow functions as an associate surgeon within an accredited private ambulatory surgery center. The fellow conducts their own new patient consultations, quotes discounted surgical fees, schedules surgical blocks, and performs operations as the primary surgeon with an attending available in the building or scrubbing in for critical portions.
   - *Why it matters:* This model provides unmatched preparation for private practice. You directly experience the friction of patient conversion, patient expectations, managing complications in paying patients, and postoperative revision requests.
   - *Key question to ask:* *"What is the conversion rate of fellow clinic consults to actual OR cases, and do fellows keep any portion of professional fees or billing?"*

2. **The Integrated Academic / Resident-Fellow Clinic (e.g., UTSW, Beth Israel Deaconess, UAB Connell, Pittsburgh)**
   - *How it works:* The fellow operates in a university cosmetic clinic, often sharing or supervising senior plastic surgery residents (chiefs/PGY-5s). Operative autonomy is exceptionally high because the fellow is legally credentialed as a junior faculty/clinical instructor.
   - *Why it matters:* You have near-total operative independence and often perform cases without an attending scrubbed. However, the patient population is often drawn from a resident clinic demographic (lower price point) rather than the ultra-high-net-worth private clientele.
   - *Key question to ask:* *"How are cosmetic cases divided between the aesthetic fellow and the PGY-6/chief residents, and who holds first pick of complex facelifts or primary rhinoplasties?"*

3. **The High-Yield Apprenticeship / Pure Mentorship Model (e.g., H/K/B Cosmetic Surgery, Belcara Health, Advanced Cosmetic Surgery, Roseville)**
   - *How it works:* No independent fellow clinic exists. The fellow spends 100% of their operative time scrubbing alongside master surgeons. The fellow acts as first assistant on 800–1,400 cases across the year.
   - *Why it matters:* While autonomy as primary surgeon is lower, the sheer exposure to surgical mastery, rapid problem solving, complex revision cases, and luxury boutique operations is unmatched. You see how world-class surgeons handle bleeding, difficult anatomy, and unhappy patients in real time.
   - *Key question to ask:* *"By month 6, what percentage of the case is the attending handing over to the fellow (e.g., raising flaps, SMAS dissection, lateral breast pillars, closure)?"*

4. **The Boutique Non-Surgical & Athletic Body Contouring Model (e.g., Neinstein Plastic Surgery)**
   - *How it works:* Hyper-focused on specialized aesthetic domains (e.g., awake tumescent VASER liposuction, deep plane athletic contouring, plasma skin tightening).
   - *Why it matters:* Extreme repetition in an in-demand, modern niche with extensive social media, branding, and luxury customer experience training.

---

### 2. Clinical Emphasis Matrix: Matching Your Career Goals

| Target Career Niche | Best-in-Class Fellowship Models | Critical Technical Milestones to Probe |
|:---|:---|:---|
| **Facial Rejuvenation & Deep-Plane Facelifting** | MEETH, DPSI, Quatela Center, Somenek+Pittman, Connell (UC Irvine / UAB) | Deep plane SMAS entry, ligament release, platysmaplasty, preservation blepharoplasty, structural fat grafting volume. |
| **Primary & Revision Rhinoplasty** | PSI Miami (Davis), DPSI (Rohrich), UTSW (Kenkel/Adams), LA Aesthetic (Calvert/Chopra) | Open vs closed approach, structural vs preservation rhinoplasty, rib graft harvesting, dynamic nasal airway surgery. |
| **Aesthetic Breast & Revision Surgery** | ACPS Houston, CaloAesthetics, Wall Center, H/K/B, Hedden Gunn | Mastopexy-augmentation algorithms, dual-plane conversion, capsulectomy, mesh/ADM use, explant and auto-augmentation. |
| **High-Definition Liposuction & Body Contouring** | Neinstein Plastic Surgery, Wall Center, North Texas, Advanced Aesthetics | Ultrasound-assisted (VASER), power-assisted (MicroAire), RF/plasma tightening (Renuvion), SAFE liposuction, gluteal grafting safety protocols. |
| **Academic / Departmental Practice** | UTSW, BIDMC Harvard, Cleveland Clinic, Pittsburgh, UAB Connell | Clinical research output, resident teaching credentials, institutional grant/clinical trial navigation. |

---

### 3. The Unspoken Private Practice Curriculum: What to Investigate

When speaking privately with current fellows, residents often ask about operative volume and forget to investigate the business mechanics that determine whether they succeed in post-fellowship practice:
1. **Practice Financial Literacy:** Does the fellowship director teach you how to analyze profit-and-loss statements, negotiate ASC facility fees, understand anesthesia contracts, and calculate cost-per-lead in digital advertising?
2. **Staff Leadership & Human Resources:** Are you exposed to hiring coordinators, managing scrub technicians, training medical assistants, and handling patient grievance procedures?
3. **Consultation Psychology & Conversion:** Do you watch how senior attendings explain pricing, discuss complications, handle difficult body dysmorphia patients, and politely decline candidates who are poor surgical risks?
4. **Post-Fellowship Restrictive Covenants:** Does the practice have a restrictive covenant or non-compete radius that would prevent you from accepting a job in that metropolitan market after training?

---

### 4. Fellow Outreach Etiquette: How to Get Unvarnished Truths

- **Timing:** Reach out between November and January. Current fellows have completed 4–6 months of training and have an accurate, unvarnished picture of case volume and autonomy.
- **Channel:** Professional email or a brief, respectful Instagram DM to their professional account.
- **Framing:** Never ask generic questions like *"Do you like the program?"* (The answer is always yes). Instead, ask concrete, objective questions:
  - *"How many primary facelifts or breast augmentations did you log as primary surgeon last month?"*
  - *"When you operate in the fellow clinic, is an attending scrubbed in the room, unscrubbed in the room, or across the hall?"*
  - *"If a fellow clinic patient has a hematoma or infection at 2:00 AM, what is the exact institutional protocol for taking them back to the OR?"*
  - *"What percentage of your time is spent in non-surgical aesthetics (Botox, filler, lasers, Morpheus8) versus the operating room?"*
  - *"Looking back at your rank list, does this program deliver what was promised on interview day?"*
